## Gothenburg

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

*Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

Here in Gothenburg we have been trying to execute your orders for four years now. Although the result has not been what we expected when we came here, still, by your mercy alone something has been achieved. Actually, everything in spiritual life depends on the mercy of the spiritual master, and I think that this is what we have begun to realize. You have been trying to teach us to become faithful, obedient “dogs,” sitting at the master’s door, waving our tails, ready to serve him at any moment. Everybody knows that in order to train uncivilized “dogs,” to sit nicely and to obey orders, sometimes-tough methods are required. But we do not mind as long as you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, are the master training us in devotional service to Lord Kṛṣṇa, the Supreme Personality of Godhead. In that position we are willing to face whatever difficulty may appear on our way. Actually, by your grace the difficulties we might experience become the cause of great realization, which in turn arouses transcendental, ecstatic feelings within our heart. You went through so many difficulties, Śrīla Prabhupāda, just to save us *mlecchas.* So what are the tiny, tiny problems we are facing? Nothing, nothing at all. So, dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, we are simply praying at your divine lotus feet: Please go on training us; smash our false ego, destroy our puffed-up nature, and make us fully dependent on you alone. And also we beg you to please have some little patience with these fallen, obstinate, so-called servants of yours. *Cakhu-dāna dilo yei janme janme prabhu sei*. All glories, all glories to you, Śrīla Prabhupāda.

*Your servants in Gothenburg.*

**Disciples of Śrīla Prabhupāda**

Prapūjaka dāsa

**Grand disciples**

Śikhaṇḍinī-devī dāsī / Pañcavatī-devī dāsī / Bhakta Magnus / Jāhnavī-dāsī

## Guatemala

*Dearmost Śrīla Prabhupāda,*  
Please accept our completely insignificant but reverent obeisances at your divine lotus feet.

As the years go by, the vicissitudes of life have being polishing our coarse comprehension of spiritual values. Gross desires, impersonalism, immaturity, and sentimentalism block, like clouds, the diaphanous vision of the Absolute Truth. And, on this occasion, on talking of spiritual truths, I want to refer specifically to the understanding of your lofty position at the summit of Lord Gaurāṅa’s *saṅkīrtana* movement. Little by little, gradually and for all persons, your colossal image is emerging in the center of all religious principles in this age.

For those of us who didn’t have the fortune of meeting you personally, the reading of your biography and the living testimony of your books, letters, lectures and instructions reveal in a overwhelming way the unique and absolute quality of your position and activities.

You are such a saint! And nobody in history could excel you in wisdom and compassion! Even in the most trivial, common affair, you gave the perfect answer and solution, seeing it from the perspective of your purest Kṛṣṇa consciousness. What to speak of your rendering of the highest truth, Kṛṣṇa Himself! You defeated all loose, materialistic conceptions about God and taught us that the real way to please the Supreme Lord is unconditional surrender to Him. So far the atheists, impersonalists and speculators are concerned, you strip them naked, exhibiting their nasty features. Prabhupāda! We owe everything to you: Kṛṣṇa! The holy names! *Prasādam!* Everything, down to the least rule of hygiene...

In your books, you gave to your present-day followers and for all posterity the standard by which we are expected to act and the measure by which we may judge others. Nobody can deceive your true follower - even one claiming to be one of your own followers yet distorting the pure, direct, and sublime knowledge you left us.

On your order, we are back in this country, Guatemala, returning to your movement its dignity and once again delivering your message to the suffering people. In the last G.B.C. resolutions you personally passed in 1977, you approved the opening of a Hare Kṛṣṇa center in Guatemala. Unfortunately our ISKCON camp was officially canceled here in 1980, causing you sorrow. But now we are vindicating and struggling hard, fighting in courts to open up again your ISKCON and set free seven million conditioned spirit souls - proclaiming you as their savior and the most glorious devotee of the Lord in the whole history.

We may be fallen, but still, on this auspicious celebration of your Vyāsa-pūjā, we solemnly declare ourselves your followers, servants for all eternity. All glories to you, Śrīla Prabhupāda!

*Your grateful servants at Guatemala,*

Aniruddha dāsa / Akrūra dāsa / Rocanā-devī dāsī / Subhadrā-devī dāsī / Bṛhat-śloka / Bharata Ṛṣi / Nitāi / Rūpa-mañjari dāsa / Urukrama dāsa / Bhakta Estuardo / Bhakta Carlos / Silvia / Kuntī

## Guyana

*Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

Please accept our humble obeisances in the dust of your lotus feet.

The observance of this most blessed even today, Śrī Vyāsa-pūjā, especially reminds us of Śrīla Bhaktivinoda Ṭhākura’s prediction about the divine mission of your appearance in this world for the purpose of fulfilling the vision of Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu: *“pṛthivīte āche yata nāgarādi grāma sarvatra pracāra, mora haibe nāma.”* \[*CB Antya-khaṇḍa* 4.126\] Therefore we have firm faith and conviction that this world mission which you have established, ISKCON, is your very self, and we accept as the absolute truth your declaration that it is simply a hallucination to attempt to perform devotional service outside of your ISKCON movement.

You have often given us the analogy of the sticks that cannot easily be broken when held together in one bundle but are easily broken if taken separately. In this way you want us to understand the vital importance of working cooperatively within your mission and consolidating our efforts for attracting all the fallen conditioned souls who are suffering the onslaught of Kali-yuga to come and take shelter in the house you built, which is capable of accommodating the whole world.

On this most auspicious and blessed day, we are fervently praying to you for your guidance and blessings that we may be able to give up the desires for all forms of sense gratification and develop only the desire for surrendering ourselves fully at your lotus feet. May we desire to unflinchingly execute the orders of your duly appointed representatives especially in regard to the beating of your *bṛhat-mṛdaṅga*, so that your divine glories may be heard and sung in every town and village all over the world. Jagat *Guru* Śrīla Prabhupāda Ki Jaya! ISKCON *vartamāna-ācāryas ki jaya!* ISKCON G.B.C. *ki jaya!*

*We beg to remain, your eternal servants of Guyana Yātrā,*

**Disciples of Śrīla Prabhupāda**

Bhūtādi dāsa

## Hawaii

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

*Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

Please accept our most humble obeisances in the dust of your lotus feet.

You one time said, “There is no happiness to be found in this material world, but if it could be found, it would be found in Hawaii.” So here we are, attempting to serve you in such a heavenly place, where the illusion of material miseries is so much veiled. Practically speaking, we have seen so many casualties, even among our own men, take place here. Therefore we pray for your special mercy and beg that you give us the transparent vision to see through *māyā’s* glitter. You are so kind that you are trying to save all the fallen souls in this decadent age of Kali. There has never been, and there will never be, a personality as great as yourself. We therefore pray that you always allow us to remain sheltered in the dust of your lotus feet.

*Your loving servants in the Hawaiian Islands,*

## Hillsborough

*yasya deve parā bhaktir*  
*yathā deve tathā gurau*  
*tasyaite kathitā hy arthāḥ*  
*prakāśante mahātmanaḥ*

**”Only unto those great souls who have implicit faith in both the Lord and the spiritual master are all the imports of Vedic knowledge automatically revealed.” (*****Śvetāśvatara Upaniṣad 6.23*****)**

*Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

This year has been one of understanding the necessity of simple faith in your words and instructions and the necessity of giving up all trust in our mundane intelligence. You have instructed us time and time again about the necessity of having faith in the distribution of your books as the most important program in the Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement. You have stated that success in all other programs will come if one simply distributes your books. This year you have given us some practical realization of this fact.

By your transcendental arrangement, we found ourselves alone in North Carolina in the beginning of this year. We had practically no *lakṣmī,* no place to stay, or any other facilities. All that we had were some cases of your books. Being put into such a situation where we had no devotees to manage, no so-called problems to solve, we were forced to take shelter of the distribution of your books as our only hope in life. Somehow or other, at the end of the year we find ourselves here in North Carolina with many devotees, a temple, a farm, a large following amongst the local people, and many other devotional opulences.

How has this happened? We certainly did not do anything but distribute your books and arrange for the distribution of your books. Materially it doesn’t make sense; but spiritually it works.

I am simply praying to you to remember this important instruction that you have given me this year through these experiences.

*Your servants at New Goloka Mandira,*

## Hobart

*Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

Śrīla Viśvanātha Cakravartī Ṭhākura instructs us in his famous prayers for the spiritual master as follows:

*yasya prasādād bhagavat-prasādo*  
*yasyāprasādān an gatiḥ kuto ‘pi*  
*dhyāyan stuvaṁs tasya yaṣas tri-sandhyaṁ*  
*vande guroḥ śrī-caraṇāravindam*

By the satisfaction of the spiritual master, the Supreme Personality of Godhead becomes satisfied. And by not satisfying the spiritual master, there is no chance of being promoted to the plane of Kṛṣṇa consciousness. I should, therefore, meditate and pray for his mercy three times a day and offer my respectful obeisance unto him, my spiritual master.

Your Divine Grace, Śrīla Prabhupāda, you are the most worshipable personality to grace our presence. If we have any attachment for the Supreme Personality of Godhead, Lord Śrī Kṛṣṇa, it is you who have awakened it in us. This confidential knowledge of our Lord was not available to us until you blessed our lives with your appearance. Admittedly, there was, in the West, an abundance of literature on Hinduism and other aspects of Eastern philosophy that made some mention of Kṛṣṇa, but as the Supreme Lord says in *Bhagavad-gītā* (7.25):

*nāhaṁ prakāśaḥ sarvasya*  
*yogamāyā-samāvṛtaḥ*

**”I am never manifest to the foolish and unintelligent. For them I am covered by My eternal creative potency** ***yogamāyā.”*** **Factually, these fools were not even writing about Kṛṣṇa, but about their misguided conceptions of Him. However, as Lord Kṛṣṇa Himself reveals,**

*bhaktyā mām abhijānāti*  
*yāvān yaś cāsmi tattvataḥ*  
*tato māṁ tattvato jñātvā*  
*viśate tad-anantaram*

**”One can understand Me as I am only by devotional service. And when one is in full consciousness of Me by such devotion, he can enter into My kingdom.” (18.55)**

So, my dear lord and master, you appeared, most pure devotee, *jagad-guru,* beckoning us to abandon all our mental concoctions and artificial religiosity and come back home to Goloka Vṛndāvana. It is the grossest understatement to say we have no qualification to do so, but, out of your unlimited mercy, you are picking us up from the gutter of the material world and so lovingly instructing us how to behave like devotees of Kṛṣṇa. In your personal example you perfectly exhibited all the transcendental qualities necessary to associate with the Supreme Lord, and with the optimum of patience, you encouraged us in our feeble attempts to follow you.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, we pray that we can cling to your lotus feet and in turn give others an opportunity to bathe in the ocean of love you manifest for your *Guru* Mahārāja and Lord Śrī Kṛṣṇa.

*Your unworthy servants,*

**Disciples of Śrīla Prabhupāda and present ISKCON ācāryas**

Bṛhaspati dāsa / Prabhu dāsa / Veṇudhāra-devī dāsī / Gauravāṇī

## Hong Kong

*Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,*Please accept our most humble obeisances in the dust of your lotus feet.

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

*mūkaṁ karoti vācālaṁ*  
*paṅuṁ laṅghayate girim*  
*yat-kṛpā tam ahaṁ vande*  
*śrī-guruṁ dīna-tāraṇam*

As the years go by, preaching in the Oriental countries, we are realizing that whatever success is coming has nothing to do with our own meager qualifications. To convince the *śūnyavādīs* to give up their impersonal and voidist misconceptions, which are ingrained so deeply within their hearts, and to again surrender to Lord Kṛṣṇa, the Supreme Personality of Godhead, is completely beyond our capabilities. But when we periodically stand back objectively we see to our great amazement that the great preaching challenge is being met, and sincere Chinese men and women are coming forward to become staunch devotees of Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu.

How is this possible? It is possible only because you are directly present with your intense desire to see Kṛṣṇa consciousness spread throughout the entire world. In the First Canto of *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam*, you have written of your beloved spiritual master, “He lives forever by his divine instructions, and the follower lives with him.” We are also having this deep realization of you, Śrīla Prabhupāda. By following your divine instructions, no matter how difficult the situation may be, your Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement is guaranteed to take root and expand unlimitedly.

As the perfect spiritual master, you have constructed a house furnished with *bhakti,* which is providing us soothing shelter from the violent storm of this Kali-yuga. Because you are such an expert architect, Śrīla Prabhupāda, you have built the foundation for this house so strongly that nothing can disturb this fortress for the next 10,000 years. You have not only arranged for this wonderful house in which the whole world can live, but by your personal example you have taught everyone living within house the art of molding their lives in such a way that their every second is utilized in glorifying the Supreme Personality of Godhead.

Therefore, Śrīla Prabhupāda, we are humbly begging you to allow us to serve you more and more perfectly. We are not so foolish to think that we can ever repay you for the wonderful gifts you have given us, but we are praying that by translating and printing all of your books into the Chinese language and then distributing them to China’s one billion fallen conditioned souls, this will in some way please Your Divine Grace.

*We beg to remain, your eternal servants,*

## Houston

Prabhupāda, you came inside my heart.  
The path of righteousness you did chart  
Across the darkness of which I am a part.  
You are the most talented artist, and love is the art.

Prabhupāda, you dried the tears from my eyes  
And rescued the Truth from an ugly disguise  
Which was imposed by pale minds who propagate lies.  
In the whole world, you alone were wise.

Prabhupāda, you are my first true friend.  
Others broke into pieces, when you did not even bend.  
You accepted me in spite of how much I sinned  
And gave to me what others can’t even begin to lend.

Prabhupāda, the holiest person that could be,  
I am a desert and you are a sea.  
Your heart is a treasure chest you open for free  
And allow to be plundered by poor men like me.

Prabhupāda, take me to Vṛndāvana, where you stay, I pray.  
Don’t leave me; I’ll die before the end of the day.  
Moments are millenniums since you’ve gone away.  
I’m your child crying in the woods, and I’ve gone astray.

Prabhupāda, I see you in Vṛndāvana with a golden hue,  
Translating in your room between midnight and two,  
Or on your roof, the holy dhāma expanding before your view.  
Imagining your pastimes I feel my spirit renew.

Prabhupāda sits chanting japa; he’s so peaceful and still.  
And Vṛndāvana sunrise breaking across the windowsill,  
Sādhus in cādars walking by, and the morning air has a chill.  
Some cows grazing calmly on a nearby hill.

Prabhupāda, on a dusty Vṛndāvana road,  
Walking at will into Kṛṣṇa’s abode,  
Which through literary visions to the world you showed  
As the seeds of saintliness in the heart you sowed.

Prabhupāda, walking in the Ramaṇa-retī sand,  
Even your causal movements are a divine plan.  
When will all my actions come under you command?

Prabhupāda, under the Vṛndāvana sky,  
Your beauty enhanced by the tears that you cry  
Out of the ecstasy of that love which can never die -  
The same love you showed me, I don’t know why.

Prabhupāda, gazing across the sacred Yamunā River,  
The holy name vibrates in your heart, and your body quivers  
With the same love of which you are the greatest giver.  
Please bless me that your mercy to others I may always deliver.

Prabhupāda, passing by forests of fragrant Tulasī trees,  
Your mind is a temple in which thoughts are on their knees,  
Reciting prayers in glorification of your Deities.  
To let me enter that temple, I am begging you, please.

## Ibadan

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

*My dear Śrīla Prabhupāda*  
Please accept my humble obeisances. On this auspicious occasion of your appearance we have another occasion to remember you and all the wonderful things you taught us. Before meeting Your Divine Grace we knew nothing; but on the basis of higher authority you have revealed everything, first about the Supreme Personality of Godhead, then about His representative, and finally about ourselves. Just because of our ignorance you left Vṛndāvana, where you were relishing the pastimes of Kṛṣṇa, and came to this dark world of meat-eaters.

Here we were committing all sorts of sinful actions, and by your unlimited mercy we were able to give them up and follow the right path to Kṛṣṇa, and also we can see that by your glance we are gradually being free from false ego, an intense desire for name, fame, profit and distinction, a lack of pure love for your divine lotus feet. Please, we are always praying at your lotus feet every day to surrender more and more. Because we are like petals in your lotus hand, please kindly inspire us how to execute our duty properly. Actually, we have no knowledge by which we can glorify Your Divine Grace, so in this way kindly forgive us, because we can’t fully understand your divine appearance in this world.

We can see many times while on *saṅkīrtana* some people will be arguing, but immediately when they open the *Back to Godhead* and see your divine picture, they will surrender. So in this we beg you to permit us to spread Kṛṣṇa consciousness to the whole world. We, your servants named under these insignificant words, are begging for your mercy to surrender more and more to your lotus feet. Jaya Śrīla Prabhupāda!!! Jaya Jagad-*guru*!!!

*Your insignificant servants*

Ādi-deva dāsa / Bhakta Oko / Bhakta John / Bhakta Christian / Bhakta Aliyu / Bhakta Augustine / Bhakta Paul / Candrāvali-devī dāsī

## Indonesia

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

*Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

We have nothing of worth to offer you on your appearance day but what you have given us. We hope you will accept our humble offering, just as the Ganges is worshiped by an offering of Ganges water. By your personal association and by your teachings recorded in your books you have saved countless fallen conditioned souls such as ourselves from the clutches of material existence. No one can sufficiently praise your glorious activities in establishing ISKCON and spreading Kṛṣṇa consciousness all over the world, a work that is going on with increasing vigor everywhere. You have set the perfect example of how one should make the order of the spiritual master his life and soul. Just as you took the order of Śrīla Bhaktisiddhānta as your life’s mission and by the grace of Kṛṣṇa achieved success, so we pray that by your mercy we may become fully dedicated to carrying out your orders to publish and distribute your books far and wide, in as many languages as there are, to convince people of every race and creed to take up the nonsectarian process of Kṛṣṇa consciousness by becoming devotees, and to establish places of worship of Śrī Śrī Gaura-Nitāi and Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Kṛṣṇa in every home and in every town and village throughout the world.

Just five years ago there were practically no devotees in Indonesia, no temples of ISKCON, and none of your books available in the Indonesian language - a sad situation for the 160 million conditioned souls who had taken their births as human beings in Indonesia. Now by your mercy almost 300,000 copies of your books have been published in the Indonesian language, there are two nice temples in Jakarta and Denpasar, and there are hundred of devotees who have taken up the blissful process of Kṛṣṇa consciousness which you have brought to the world through your books and your disciples.

Although we are unqualified, out of your kindness you have accepted us as your disciples and grand disciples, and you are guiding us toward the perfection of life, Kṛṣṇa consciousness. You appearance in this world is the most significant event, a brilliant flash of light in the dense darkness of Kali-yuga. We make our humble offering in your glorification on this anniversary of your appearance in this world, and we pray that we may always be engaged in your service.

*Your unworthy servants of Indonesia Yātrās,*

**Disciples of Śrīla Prabhupāda**

## Ireland

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

*Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

Please accept our humble obeisances. “If a blind man is walking down the wrong path, how can a gentleman allow him to continue?” you said, and so you came. In spite of the heavy sea, in spite of hellish Lower East Side experiences, you kept faith in the instruction of your spiritual master:

*yāre dekha, tāre kaha kṛṣṇa-upadeśa*  
*āmāra ājñāya guru haña tāra’ ei deśa*

In London you asked all the book distributors from Germany, ”And what is this *Kṛṣṇa-upadeśa?*” And when we didn’t really know the answer, you spoke: “*Sarva-dharmān parityajya...*” This is the message of Kṛṣṇa, and you have gone out of your way to distribute this message of Kṛṣṇa to us fallen souls.

If you had not stopped the blind men, they would have stumbled straight into their graves. Another birth, another death - “*saṁsāra-dāvānala-līḍha-loka*.” That is your kindness.

To repay you, let us therefore pray today that we may become perfect as you are perfect, perfect sons of a perfect father. This would be your pleasure.

Promising to do better than before in this coming year - by your mercy -

*Your servants of ISKCON Ireland,*

**Disciples of Śrīla Prabhupāda**

## Israel

Oh my beloved *Guru*deva, *Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

I offer my humble obeisances to your lotus feet. What can I say? I am useless and unqualified. Your causeless mercy is the most wonderful thing imaginable.

I am very sorry that I have not done anything worthy for your pleasure. I pray to Your Divine Grace to please grant me that I may be able to do some pleasing service to you. That is my only request. There is nothing else of value to be obtained anywhere. To please the spiritual master is supreme, the highest bliss and knowledge.

Although you left this world almost seven years ago - we still have you deep in our hearts. We will never forget you.

Please stay with us always.

*Your eternal servants in Israel,*

**Disciples of Śrīla Prabhupāda**

Dānavīra dāsa / Gaṇāvatāra dāsa

## Italy

*Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

You picked me up from an ocean of unfathomable darkness. I remember how the confusion of my mind had made intolerable any thought of survival in this body. It is said that death is that time when the soul cannot remain in the body because the situation has become intolerable.

Ignorance had become so deep that to continue to live was for me a difficult task. You have a special quality, Śrīla Prabhupāda: You take something miserable and detestable and make some use out of it. Even I who detested myself and had a particular liking for the feelings of a miserable being - by showing that my mind, words and body could be used in the service of Kṛṣṇa, you forced me to gain some hope. Actually I did not want to have any hope. So strong was my fear due to envy and frustration that I was escaping from hope. But you forced me. The beautiful sight of your picture in the *Bhagavad-gītā* forced me. The sweetness of your glance when I first saw you in Śrīdhāma Māyāpur forced me. The knowledge you gave through your divine books forced me. Now I feel a little grateful - but it is a long way from becoming pure. Pure means I can recognize who you are. Pure means totally engaged and no nonsense.

My prayer now is that you tell Kṛṣṇa to make any nonsense intolerable to me, that lack of Kṛṣṇa consciousness may become the unbearable state of existence I should fear and escape.

My life is short and memory unclear, but your transcendental potency, Śrīla Prabhupāda, walks over space and time, and when *māyā* attacks you are there to remind me: Just let Kṛṣṇa protect you. That is why without you there is no hope. I would forget everything - Rādhā-Kṛṣṇa, Lord Caitanya, the devotees, the books, the preaching - everything. The tendency would be again to non-exist, the topmost for of envy to the Supreme Lord.

Once in Śrīdhāma Māyāpura, during a *darśana* in your room, I speculated that because you are the representative of Kṛṣṇa and you are constantly thinking of your disciples, I surely was under the protection of the divine energy. Your answer was, “Yes, I always think of you, but if you do not think of me, what is the use?”

After you departed from our vision a new definition of devotional service became dear to my understanding: “activities in devotional service are Prabhupāda conscious activities.” You always said there is no need of artificial adjustment to make progress in Kṛṣṇa consciousness. It is a fact. If we remember you, we shall be compassionate, fearless, honest, strong and tolerant. We shall push this movement beyond our expectations and liberate the whole universe. A devotee should not desire to gain any kind of liberation, but there is one kind of liberation I want, Śrīla Prabhupāda - to join you and all the devotees in a place where flawless service and attachment to you lotus feet are the only reality and Kṛṣṇa and Balarāma are pleased with me.

Your servants from Italy Yātrā

## Kenya

Blessed than heaven, sweeter than May,  
When you appeared that holy day,  
Bringing new hope to a dying place,  
Our dearmost well-wisher, Your Divine Grace.

From the ocean of nescience you did save,  
The path back home so clearly you paved,  
The footsteps of saints did you retrace,  
O perfect Ācārya, Your Divine Grace.

To the darkest regions of hell you came  
With mercy divine in showers to rain.  
Hardship and danger did you embrace,  
Our lord and savior, Your Divine Grace.

Unleashment of sin now taking its hold,  
The mission of Godhead did you unfold,  
With seeds of bhakti did you replace,  
Most merciful master, Your Divine Grace.

Giving us vision, restoring our sight,  
Saving us from our perilous plight,  
Bent towards hell, this human race,  
The only hope, Your Divine Grace.

O evangelic angel, give us the light,  
Your words are our candle to fain Kali’s night.  
Please give us the strength to take up your mace,  
Dear Jagad-guru, Your Divine Grace.

You showed us the Lord within every heart  
And gave us rebirth, a fresh new start,  
Reactions to karma did you erase,  
Our all-loving father, Your Divine Grace.

Caitanya’s message was brought by you,  
As pure and sweet as morning dew,  
Beyond mundane realms of time and space,  
The Lord’s dearmost servant, Your Divine Grace.

Exposing the cheaters and rascals as fraud,  
Eternally servant and never the Lord,  
You challenged asuras and atheists did face,  
Oh, glorious indeed, Your Divine Grace.

O merciful master, please give us your hand,  
Guide us back home to that eternal land.  
Save we most fallen from wretched disgrace;  
Your mercy’s our life, Your Divine Grace.

Blessed than heaven, sweeter than May,  
When you appeared that holy day,  
Golden of hue and lotus face,  
Our dearmost well-wisher, Your Divine Grace.

Your worthless servants of ISKCON Kenya,

**NAIROBI**

## Korsnas gard

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

**”I offer my respectful obeisances unto His Divine Grace A.C. Bhaktivedanta Swami Prabhupāda, who is very dear to Lord Kṛṣṇa, having taken shelter at His lotus feet.  Our respectful obeisances are unto you, O spiritual master, servant of Sarasvatī Gosvāmī. You are kindly preaching the message of Lord Caitanyadeva and delivering the Western countries, which are filled with impersonalism and voidism.”**

*My Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

Please accept my most humble obeisances at your divine lotus feet. Representing New Rādhā-kuṇḍa, I make some reflections. The number of your disciples is decreasing here. Is it that your disciples are falling away? Some may have left, but what actually is happening is that your movement continuously is expanding. Thus we find your disciples spread all over the place, preaching Kṛṣṇa consciousness on the front lines. And here at New Rādhā-kuṇḍa, your preaching is continuously expanding through the immense book production that is going on. Your disciples and grand disciples are pushing on to translate and print your books in all kinds of languages. Just now the translation and publication of your books have been completed in the Swedish language. That is just the start. Now the publication of several other languages is carrying on. I think this is the actual offering of New Rādhā-kuṇḍa unto you. I can just look with amazement at what is going on.

You may not be physically present, but you certainly are spiritually. Thus the movement carries on to expend here. Through every page of your books you are still preaching to us. “Surrender unto Kṛṣṇa and give up that sense gratification. Instead, enjoy the real pleasure of pure, unmotivated, and uninterrupted devotional service.” The great souls are transcendentally present through their instructions. Thus by attentively listening to their message we still receive the benefit of their association. That is your miracle - not only to start this movement but also to enable it to dynamically expand in spite of your physical absence. You never claimed to be God, nor the controller, but you always presented yourself as the servant of your spiritual master. Thus you taught us by your practical example, as well as through transcendental instructions, how to surrender.

You already showed your greatness when you were here. Now when your *vapuḥ* is gone you are still here in your *vāṇī*. And thus more and more conditioned souls are continuously being attracted to this path shown by you. Those who are not serious in their endeavor fall away. *Daivī hy eṣā guṇamayī mama māyā duratyayā:* “This divine energy of Mine, consisting of the three modes of material nature, is difficult to overcome. But those who have surrendered unto Me can easily cross beyond it.” To surrender unto Kṛṣṇa means to surrender unto the spiritual master. “The spiritual master is to be honored as much as the Supreme Lord, because he is the most confidential servitor of the Lord. This is acknowledged in all revealed scriptures and followed by all authorities. Therefore I offer my respectful obeisances unto the lotus feet of such a spiritual master, who is a bona fide representative of Śrī Hari.” Being the direct representative of Kṛṣṇa, you are able to deliver us conditioned souls from our illusion. This provided, we take advantage of your transcendental association in your instructions and teachings. Kṛṣṇa says, “Surrender unto me.” Where is Kṛṣṇa? Here in your books at every page you are revealing Kṛṣṇa to us, not theoretically, but practically. Kṛṣṇa is there. Just see how wonderfully your movement is spreading all over the world! Because you are the transparent via medium, you gave us Kṛṣṇa. And by your mercy we are still receiving Kṛṣṇa from you. Therefore any one of us who seriously desires the supreme perfection must take your books seriously. We must read them, live them, print them, and distribute them. To give these books out means to give out your mercy, Śrīla Prabhupāda. Prabhupāda, you are in our hearts waiting for us to surrender unto your mission. Prabhupāda, you did so much for us. So let us on this auspicious occasion surrender our lives unto you and give up all self-motivated considerations. May we just simply devote our lives to help fulfill your desire, to print and distribute your books in all languages of the world.

*We beg to remain, your humble servants,*

## Lagos

To the one who showed the way,  
Who lit up my sordid day,

Who cast a silver arrow  
To fell my woe and sorrow.

Any day now I’m going to make the grade  
To satisfy the master, whose disciple I was made,  
To give up my maya, as an illusion of the mind.  
Any other fetish will have to rescind.

Now it seems as though ages have passed,  
And the sediment of sin has still not lapsed.  
And I foolishly question the process, not the person,  
Now that my pride in self has blocked the way of reason.

The words of my guru have pricked my conscious,  
Yet the muck of past misconduct interrupts the process,  
Yet the filth of delusion stains my soul’s dedication.  
The wrong I thought right has marred my devotion.

If it was you I loved, would not the illusion disappear  
As a cloud overhead on to make the sky clear?  
I would one day pray to have a glimpse of the Lord  
If He could deem this soul worthy, a vision to be offered.

What little attempt I make, what little vision I have!  
And yet I demand that my soul be saved,  
And yet I proclaim myself to know all that is.  
What wonder the Lord put me out of that divine realm of His.

As you had strived so hard, so strong, so persistent and bold,  
May I so follow before the body grows too old.  
May I do something for you while I still live,  
A fallen soul, I, at your feet, with nothing to give.

All glories to Śrīla Prabhupāda,  
Who with the cakra-like weapon of the Bhāgavatam  
is killing the faceless, hydra headed māyāvādī monster  
And thus is releasing everyone from this material sham.

All glories to Śrīla Prabhupāda, mahā-bhāgavata,  
Whose lotus feet are the abode of the great Vaiṣṇavas,  
The brilliant touchstone that turns the heart of suckers to sādhus.  
He verily becomes the personification of the Vedas.

All glories to Śrīla Prabhupāda,  
Who is the expert physician who removes the virus of modern science.  
Teaching the pure philosophy of Bhagavad-gītā,  
He is the swift deliverer from the poisoned ocean of nescience.

All glories to Śrīla Prabhupāda,  
The esteemed traveler of the Jaladuta.  
Embracing cold and mlecchas and junkies’ jeers,  
He came to the concrete streets of kali to establish pure dharma.

All glories to Śrīla Prabhupāda,  
Who has conquered the illusion of sleep,  
Vibrating the mahā-mantra in every land and clime,  
He drives away the dreaded fear of the deep.

All glories to Śrīla Prabhupāda,  
Whose Bhaktivedanta purports release everyone from the shackles of kāla.  
Revealing the qualities of the absolute reality,  
He infuses ecstatic love for the kīrtana of Śrī Gaurasundara.

All glories to Śrīla Prabhupāda, jagad-guru,  
Who has come down in the line of Vyāsa and Lord Gaurāṅga.  
From his lotus lips flows the nectar of transcendental poetry,  
And he is always merged in chanting the glories of Śrī Bhaktisiddhānta.

Let all that live glorify this paramahaṁsa  
Who lived among the hippies, establishing real peace,  
And let the day of his descent be a day of joy,  
For by his mercy everyone merges into the ocean of eternal bliss.

*Your disciples and grand disciples in Lagos, Nigeria,*

## Laguna Beach

A wicked mind brought us into this world of misery and pain. We became absorbed in abominable activities and avoided the company of saintly men. But then you came into our lives when everything seemed hopeless, and you gave us great hope to carry on. “The spiritual future is always bright,” we heard you say.

*oṁ tad viṣṇoḥ paramaṁ padaṁ sadā*  
*paśyanti sūrayo divīva cakṣur atatam*  
*tad viprāso vipanyavo jāgṛvāṁsaḥ*  
*samindhate viṣṇor yat paramaṁ padaṁ*

But for the materialists, the future is always dark. O Śrīla Prabhupāda, may we always be engaged in your missionary activities, for it is in these activities that you are most present. Thus we can always have your association and never feel the need to go elsewhere for enlivenment or whatever. You are fully capable of satisfying all of the desires of your devotees. Although you were living in Vṛndāvana, you left that most holy place and came to this remote part of the world for the welfare of fallen souls like us. How can we ever give up serving you? We bow down to your beautiful lotus feet again and again and again.

## Lake Huntington

*Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,*  
Please accept our humble obeisances in the dust of your divine lotus feet.

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

One tiny spirit soul, one ten-thousandth the tip of a hair, blinded to reality;  
Small groups of blinded spirit souls, the best of whom could only sense bare glimmers of light,  
Can see now, by your grace,  
Not just light or shadowy speculation,  
But the form of the Lord.

*Jagannāthaḥ svāmī nayana-patha-gāmī bhavatu me.*

Śrīla Prabhupāda, during your manifest pastimes on this earthly planet, this project had not appeared yet: it was simply separated by time. Your lotus feet, which walked the earth simply to benedict her and which gave shelter to all living entities, never touched the rocky barren hills of New Puruṣottama Kṣetra. Now flowers are blooming; children are running, playing; cows grazing; all enveloped in the net of the holy name you brought here. There are blooming flowers to be offered to Lord Jagannātha; here are devotees running to the temple, and *kīrtana* within; playing out the pastimes of Lord Rāmacandra in the forest; all, including the “elders,” growing up joyfully in one room of your house, built for the whole world to live in. O Śrīla Prabhupāda, please let us never run away from this, our home.

Two cows are satisfied to eat grass and prepare to give forth sweet milk; two oxen are hauling a cart full of wood, working so hard, but with a look of serene contentment on their faces. Don’t they realize whom they are serving? O Śrīla Prabhupāda, please let us be satisfied, work hard, be content; but let us always remember we are eternally serving you.

Your grandchildren, over seventy strong, are growing up in Kṛṣṇa consciousness. *Man-manā bhava mad-bhaktaḥ:* those are your instructions for the training of these young devotees; but have they realized yet their eternal debt to you, *jagad-guru*, who has given them everything? One young child watching the animals grazing asks if the grass they eat is *prasādam*. Yes, the grass and everything here is *prasādam*, Prabhupāda’s *prasādam.* O Śrīla Prabhupāda, please let us never forget that all that we have is simply your mercy.

A young boy comes running back to his teacher, one less cookie and one less book in his hand. A huge smile on his face, he exclaims, “He gave a dollar and his address too; he said I could write and tell him all about Kṛṣṇa!” Śrīla Prabhupāda, you’ve given us everything through your instructions: how to become blissful - preaching is the essence - and how to raise these children with a joyful experience of Kṛṣṇa consciousness - let them do as the adults are doing. O Śrīla Prabhupāda, please let your instructions be the only guide in our lives.

One tiny spirit soul, eyes slowly opening, hears of the spiritual realm;  
Small groups of slowly awakening souls, cooperating to push on your glorious movement,  
Can experience the spiritual reality  
By your grace only, and a debt that can never be repaid.

## Lima

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

*guru-kṛṣṇa-rupa hāña śāstrera pramāṇe*  
*guru-rūpe kṛṣṇa-kṛpā harena bhakta-gaṇe*

**”According to the deliberate opinion of the revealed scriptures, the spiritual master is identical with Lord Kṛṣṇa. Lord Kṛṣṇa in the form of spiritual master delivers His devotees.” (*****Cc. Ādi*** **1.45)**

*Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

Please accept our most humble obeisances in the dust of your transcendental lotus feet. The above verse describes how the all-merciful Supreme Lord has manifested Himself before us by your grace, and is delivering us from material bondage and taking us to the eternal abode.

As founder*-ācārya* of this movement for Kṛṣṇa consciousness, you had to teach us everything. We did not know either what to eat, or how to eat, how long to sleep, or how to be clean, and what to speak about how to worship God and serve Him.

What you have achieved in spreading the Vedic knowledge in the world, training the Western people in the real principles of a perfect culture of Kṛṣṇa consciousness, is extraordinary and unique, even in our disciplic succession, in which great personalities and divine incarnations appeared. Due to your transcendental view and to your love for your *guru mahārāja’s* instructions, you poured out blessings without discrimination, doing what for others was impossible. Only a personality empowered by the Lord Himself can distribute the holy name and deliver the most fallen, even after his physical departure.

As you yourself taught us, “On his absence, the spiritual master’s words of direction should be the pride of the disciple (there is no difference between the *guru* and his instructions). The spiritual master is always present with the disciple by *vāṇī,* his words.”

Thus, it is a fact that you are still guiding us and that through your books and your sincere followers you are spreading the holy names around the world.

We are only your instruments, and our success depends on how we cooperate and stay united with each other, leaving behind all our desires and personal considerations, and surrendering completely to fulfill your mission.

So, for some you may be as an eternal father and for others a transcendental grandfather, Śrīla Prabhupāda, but please always remain in our hearts, because only in that way will the desires of Lord Kṛṣṇa ever be manifested here.

*Your 108 servants of Lima Mandira,*

## Long Island

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

*Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

All glories to your lotus feet, which are the shelter of all living entities. You are the world *ācārya* and savior of the human society, so deeply afflicted by the age of Kali. In the forest of Jhārikaṇḍa Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu engaged jungle beasts in chanting and dancing to the holy names of Kṛṣṇa. Similarly, as an empowered associate of Lord Gaurāṅga, you have engaged the beastlike human beings of this world in chanting, dancing, feasting and philosophy. When asked to show miracles, you pointed to your American disciples and said, “These are my miracles.”

We cannot measure our debt to you, Śrīla Prabhupāda. You have advised us, “The debt of the spiritual master can never be repaid, but if you want to try, then just preach.” Preaching is the essence of pleasing you, and satisfaction of the *guru* is the key to success in spiritual life. Please accept us as your servants. Your mercy is all we are made of.

*Your disciples and grand disciples,*

## Los Angeles

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

*jaya śrī-kṛṣṇa caitanya prabhu nityānanda*  
*śrī advaita gadādhara śrīvāsādi gaura-bhakta vṛnda*

*hare kṛṣṇa hare kṛṣṇa kṛṣṇa kṛṣṇa hare hare*  
*hare rāma hare rāma rāma rāma hare hare*

*jaya prabhupāda! jaya prabhupāda!*  
*jaya prabhupāda! jaya prabhupāda!*

**Who are you, Prabhupāda?**

Can we ever really know or understand  
your transcendental position?  
Perhaps, by the grace of the Lord,  
we will come closer by this inadequate rendition.

Who are you, Prabhupāda?

You are mercy, unsurpassed,  
Coming from an unlimited source.  
Mercy that will last  
And keep us on the course.

You are abhaya, fearless and bold,  
More than the rest.  
By the order you were told  
You came to the West.

You are a most humble person,  
An “insignificant beggar,” you said  
Who is saving this nation  
Populated by the dead.

You are tolerant and kind,  
And your saintly behavior  
Leaves others behind  
Who think themselves the savior.

You are always giving divine wisdom  
Of matter and soul.  
Your books are the basis  
For life’s real goal.

You are chanting and dancing,  
Simply relishing the name.  
By your grace we are advancing.  
You are our only claim.

You are in your instructions  
And will never, ever die.  
You are eternally fixed  
In the spiritual sky.

You are ISKCON,  
A society for preaching,  
The perfect plan  
To spread Lord Caitanya’s teachings.

*You are Śrīla Prabhupāda, the master of all masters*.

*Your eternal servants of the New Dvārakā Community,*

## Malaysia

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

*Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

On this most important of festivals, we are simply remembering the *cintamani* magic mood of your 1965-1977 preaching *līlā.* We, the whole ISKCON society, orbited around you, and we, your disciples, danced like puppets to your direction. Your words were law, your pronunciations scripture, and your activities all-instructive *līlā.* The devotees, like bees anxious for honey, eagerly awaited your latest revelations in the form of your Bhaktivedanta purports, lecture tapes, and personal instructions.

Of course, there were big devotees and small devotees, but all were ants compared to you. After you left, we were dumbstruck, thinking, “The only pure devotee on the planet has left; what to do now?”

But, somehow, we got back into the rhythm again, by remembering your divine instructions. Several of your leading disciples have taken up the mind-boggling responsibilities of *ācārya*-ship and have done very well, by your divine grace. In fact, we are seeing your potency manifested through your advanced disciples. Thus the movement continues to expand and grow. What does the future hold? Only brightness, if we remember you, Śrīla Prabhupāda. And only darkness if we forget you or foolishly, madly, even slightly begin to relegate you to the past. You are our Kṛṣṇa conscious past; without you our present Kṛṣṇa consciousness has no meaning whatsoever, and you are also our only hope for the future.

Our only hope is always you, Śrīla Prabhupāda. ISKCON *will* succeed, by *your* divine grace. It’s not that we’ve become so big that we can stand independently of you. We must always remain helpless infants before you. Then only can we be successful. Just as when you were personally present, all doubts, fears and dissention were resolved in you, let us keep that mood now and forever. We want you to reappear again, Prabhupāda, in our hearts, manifest millions of times more strongly through our pure desire to receive you, for without you we have no hope whatsoever.

**Disciples of Śrīla Prabhupāda**

## Manchester

*Śrīla Prabhupāda,*  
please accept our humble obeisances at your lotus feet. Last September we were sent here to establish Kṛṣṇa consciousness, and due to your mercy only our great movement is spreading its roots in this area.

Sometimes, due to faithlessness, we do not feel the determination necessary to accomplish the task that we have been given. But by remembering and reading about your wonderful and inconceivable faith and selflessness in your struggle in the early days in New York, we feel both ashamed and inspired to try to follow your example.

Manchester is undoubtedly a great field for preaching, as so many devotees have already come from this area. We are praying for a drop of your compassion so that we can stay here to make others fortunate.

We are opening a small restaurant and preaching center just by the largest university in Europe, so we hope to make many devotees there in the next year.

By your strong desire we are sure that Kṛṣṇa consciousness will spread rapidly in the Manchester area. We hope to accomplish this very rapidly, so that you will truly be recognized as *jagad-guru,* the person that could build a house big enough for the entire world to live happily in.

Your servants in Manchester,

## Manipura

*Most worshipable Śrīla Prabhupāda,*  
Kindly allow us to take the shelter of your divine lotus feet. Kindly allow us to sing your glories louder and stronger at all times and under all circumstances. This will make us free from the bondage of material existence. This land of Narottama is heavily attacked by the waves of Kali. You alone, Śrīla Prabhupāda, can save us from such terrible waves. On this most auspicious day of your appearance, we pay our humble tribute.

## Melbourne

**”Savior of the World”**

*kaler doṣa-nidhe rājann asti hy eko mahān guṇaḥ*  
*kīrtanād eva kṛṣṇasya mukta-saṅgaḥ paraṁ vrajet*

Five thousand years ago  
The sages spoke this message quite sublime.  
They spoke of Kali-yuga as a very special time.  
Whilst it remains an ocean full of faults,  
Whilst men are lazy, misguided, and disturbed,  
The holy name of Kṛṣṇa  
Shines forth as the only light  
To save all conditioned souls  
From a painful, sinful plight.

Prabhupāda!  
As a messenger from Goloka  
You have brought a priceless gem.  
You have “imported” Hari-nāma to grace the world  
And bless all fallen men.

So long had we been sleeping  
On the lap of the witch called māyā,  
So long had we been burning up in the great saṁsāra fire,  
Our eyes were shut tight in the darkness of night,  
But you forced them wide open with spiritual light.

To show compassion on the victims  
Of this dark and fearsome place,  
Armed with Kṛṣṇa-śakti,  
Your transcendental books -  
The sword to cut through nescience -  
You smashed all demon’s theories  
With your liberating mace.

”You are not this body!”  
A simple message, true;  
But who had ever thought  
That the body wasn’t “you”?

”Kick out impersonal nonsense!  
Kṛṣṇa’s in control!  
You’re a tiny servant  
Of the complete whole!

“Give up the vain attempt for pleasure  
Through adoration, wealth, and fame.  
Please give up your sinful life -  
Chant the holy name!”

Certainly in years to come  
All men will understand your greatness.  
The flag of ISKCON will be victoriously unfurled,  
Proclaiming: “Śrīla Prabhupāda -  
The savior of the world.”

*From your disciples and grand disciples in Melbourne, Mahāprabhu Mandira*

## Mexico City

*Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

Please accept our most fallen obeisances at your lotus feet. We feel so incompetent in writing this that we are begging your mercy so that we don’t commit any offense to you.

Approaching a spiritual master is not just a fashion but a necessity for one who is seriously conscious of the material miseries and who wants to be free from them. It is the duty of such a person to approach a spiritual master. In this regard, we should note similar circumstances in *Bhagavad-gītā.* When Arjuna was perplexed by so many problems involving whether to fight or not, he accepted Lord Kṛṣṇa as his spiritual master. Therefore it is said,

*janame janame sabe pitā-mātā pāya*  
*kṛṣṇa guru nāhi mile bhaja hari ei*  
*(Bhāg. 6.16.6 purport)*

In the transmigration of the soul through different bodies, everyone, in every form of life - be it human, animal, tree or demigod - gets a father and mother. This is not very difficult. The difficulty is to obtain a bona fide spiritual master like you, Śrīla Prabhupāda; therefore the duty of a human being is to capture the opportunity to come in touch with Kṛṣṇa’s representative, the bona fide spiritual master. Under the guidance of the spiritual master, the spiritual father, one can return home, back to Godhead. So, diseased and conditioned, the living entity transmigrates throughout the universe. Sometimes he is situated in the upper planetary system and sometimes in the lower system. In this way he leads his diseased life. His disease can be cured only when he meet and follows the expert physician, the pure devotee of Lord Kṛṣṇa. If we, the conditioned souls, faithfully follow your instructions, our material disease is cured. So please, Śrīla Prabhupāda, be merciful upon us and give us shelter at your lotus feet.

*Your servants of Mexico City Mandira,*

*Guru*prasāda Swami

## Mexico Farm

*Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

Please accept our most humble obeisances at your lotus feet.

Trying to adequately glorify you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, is always an apparently impossible task. First of all, your being Kṛṣṇa’s most confidential servant means that you are on the topmost transcendental platform. Therefore, you are beyond the reach of the most fertile mind or the keenest intelligence. Secondly, as the repository of all good qualities (*sarva-devamayo guruḥ*), you are also irreproachable.

But as Kṛṣṇa says in the *Bhagavad-gītā, bhaktya mām abhijānāti:* by approaching you through devotional service, we can come to know you. So many devotees have come to the conclusion, after your external disappearance, that we are just beginning to understand your exalted position. Just as a swimming instructor may have to throw a reluctant beginner in the water to teach him to swim, you had to shock us to the stark reality of understanding our eternal relationship with Kṛṣṇa. You lead us along for some time, but you wanted us to wake up. As you stated many times, at the moment of death one is on his own. If we are determined to serve Kṛṣṇa, then Kṛṣṇa will help us. To bring us to that realization, we needed a jolt.

The most painful thing that we can think of is being separated from your merciful glance, the feeling of security in your personal presence, and every aspect of your association, which you most mercifully gave us. But that very same pain makes us strong, it forces us to take refuge in your eternal incarnation in the form of your instructions. It drives us to assimilate every last drop of nectar which is left. As the remnants of the spiritual master are called *mahā-mahā-prasādam*, now your transcendental descriptions of Kṛṣṇa and His devotional service have become sweeter and more necterean now that you have left them to us.

Bhaktivinoda Ṭhākura says that the mercy of the spiritual master is the only qualification the disciple has. Please give us your special mercy to enable us to fully understand the responsibility we have to fulfill your and Kṛṣṇa’s desire. That is the real meaning of spiritual unity, unity in the shelter of your lotus feet.

Jaya Śrīla Prabhupāda!

*Your eternal servants at Nueva Gaura-maṇḍala-bhūmi*

## Miami

*Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

Please accept our humble, prostrated obeisances at your divine lotus feet. All glories to Your Divine Grace. On this auspicious occasion of Śrī-Vyāsa-pūjā we meditate on your eternal, unlimited pastimes, which enliven us and help us stay strong in pushing on the saṅkīrtana movement of Lord Caitanya Mahāprabhu.

Prabhupāda, you are the topmost *mahātmā*, absorbed in thoughts of Lord Kṛṣṇa and never deviated from executing the desire of your spiritual master. Although there are many stumbling blocks and material distractions in our attempt to establish this ISKCON society, if we remember you and fall at your lotus feet, with deep feelings laden with heartfelt prayers, then only can we be rescued from this insurmountable *māyā.*

By reading your *Līlāmṛta*, which has been very expertly presented by a most humble servant, Śrīla Satsvarūpa dāsa Goswami, we are drawn closer and closer toward your glorious wonderful activities. The more we glorify you and remember your instructions, to that degree we can approach the ultimate goal of life, *kṛṣṇa-prema*. Śrīla Prabhupāda, please continue guiding, developing and enthusing the rapid growth of the eternal Caitanya tree. You instructed us all to become *guru,* a branch of the Caitanya tree, preaching the message of *Bhagavad-gītā* and *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam* to everyone we meet.

But without your association and divine mercy none of us can do anything; we will be completely powerless in our attempts.

We are very small, most insignificant creatures, but we want to serve you and let everyone know of your glories. If you desire, please let us develop and beatify Govinda’s Vaikuṇṭha Building, making it a place of pilgrimage where fallen conditioned souls can come and become purified by your presence.

We pray to remain eternally engaged in your transcendental loving service and to help give others the same life that you have given us.

*Your eternal servants,*

## Montreal

*Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

On this auspicious occasion of your Vyāsa-pūjā, Śrīla Prabhupāda, the devotees of Montreal Yātrā wish to offer you, our beloved preceptor, and our most humble obeisances. Please accept our poem, Your Divine Grace.

Montreal is an auspicious place,  
Since back in 1896  
Bhaktivinoda Ṭhākura’s book arrived  
And was kept in McGill University’s archives.

Then in 1967 Śrīla Kīrtanānanda Swami came  
Bringing the Supreme Lord’s glories and fame  
By opening the famous “bowling lanes”  
As the marketplace of the holy names.

Also in ‘67’s World Fair, the pavilion of religion,  
Śrīla Prabhupāda, you were there,  
Revealing to humanity what really was,  
”Man and his world,” from the point of view of God.

Then disciples like Gopāla Kṛṣṇa came,  
And your efforts did not go in vain,  
He was to take the mace in disciplic chain,  
Carrying on preaching Kṛṣṇa’s fame.

And another disciple also came, Jayapatāka was his name,  
And devotees started to complain  
About his loud chanting of the holy names,  
And you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, to him said,  
”Chant on the mountain,” and so he did.

We are pleased to tell Your Divine Grace  
That Montreal Yātrā is a wonderful place.  
By Rādhā-Manohara’s special grace,  
French people, and English too,  
Receive books and prasādam because of you.

They are coming, Śrīla Prabhupāda, and taking part,  
Pulling Lord Jagannātha’s gigantic cart.  
Chez Govinda’s is giving prasādam, FOLK, cooking classes,  
Sunday feasts, college preaching, hari-nāma,  
The French people are taking note,  
The Hare Krishnas are real nice folks.

Thank you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, for giving us this chance  
To preach Kṛṣṇa’s glories, to chant and dance.  
Please continue to bless us, Your Divine Grace,  
So we may help you continue the inundation,  
As ISKCON’s house attracts family members from all nations.

*Your fallen servants of ISKCON Montreal,*

## Morelia

*Our dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

*karṇadhāra ivāpāre*  
*bhagavān pāra-darśakaḥ*

**”You are like a captain in a great ocean, and you can direct us to our destination.” (*****Bhāg.*** **1.13.40)**

Now we have resolved all our doubts simply by meeting you, for Your Divine Grace has very kindly and clearly explained the duty of a human being.

It is our good fortune that we have been able to see you, for you cannot easily be seen by persons who have not subdued the mind or controlled the senses. We are all more fortunate to have touched with our heads the dust of your feet.

You have fortunately instructed us, and thus great favor has been bestowed upon us. We thank God, and we try to open our ears to your pure words.

We continuously offer our respectful obeisances unto your lotus feet, which are worthy to take shelter of because you shower all benedictions on the insignificant by means of the *śrī-kṛṣṇa-saṅkīrtana* of Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu, giving to all living entities the chance to be free from the material existence.

You have given us the grace of Śrī Caitanya  
Mahāprabhu. Therefore you are known as - His Divine Grace

Anyone who comes and surrenders unto you  
becomes free from all fear of material existence;  
therefore your name is Abhaya Caraṇāravinda,  
abbreviated - A.C.

You have written many books; therefore you  
deserve the title - Bhaktivedanta

You always have proven control over all your  
senses. The Kavirāja asked you if there was anything  
you wanted, and you replied, *“kuccha*  
*icchā nahi”;* “I have no desire,” therefore you  
prove to be a - Swami

For accepting and giving shelter to so many  
disciples you fully prove that you are - Prabhupāda

Because you started this Society you are known  
as the - Founder

It is said that one who is glorious in his activities  
is always living, never dies. Therefore you are  
called - *Ācārya*

And because you society is spreading the name  
of Kṛṣṇa, His qualities, His form, etc., throughout  
the whole world, it is called the International  
Society for Kṛṣṇa Consciousness,  
abbreviated - ISKCON

*Your eternal servants of Morelia Mandira, Mexico*

## Netherlands

*Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

Please accept my humble obeisances. All glories to Your Divine Grace.

Reflecting upon the many wonderful qualities of Your Divine Grace on this auspicious occasion of your Vyāsa-pūjā, one prominent feature of your personality, life, and instructions comes to mind. This feature can summarily be described as follows: You have succeeded in formulating the process of devotional service and achieving love of Kṛṣṇa in such a way that no one who studies your life and instructions can escape the conclusion that devotional service rather than material enjoyment is our natural condition of life. The essence of this conclusion is that devotional service to Kṛṣṇa is not beyond our reach and can easily be achieved and practiced by anyone who desires to have and practice it. You have made Kṛṣṇa real, a real person, a real friend, someone for whom we can develop real feelings and attraction. Throughout you life you have shown that Kṛṣṇa consciousness is not an “artificial imposition on the mind,” that it is not excruciatingly difficult to obtain it, and that it is not just obtainable by a privileged few. Rather you have shown that everyone is a spirit soul, part of Kṛṣṇa, that therefore everyone is naturally entitled to experience his relationship with Kṛṣṇa, and that the only qualification required is one’s desire to experience it. In this way you have moved religion, philosophy, and mysticism out of the dusted-over tombs of self-imposed oblivion, stuffiness, and intellectual pride. You have achieved this without, on the other hand, falling victim to the common mistake made by the popularizes of religion, who water down devotional service to the point where both devotion and service have perished, leaving only hollow phrases behind. You have made the process of devotional service simple without being superficial, profound without being stuffy, and accessible without being cheap. You have firmly established the fact that although devotional service to Kṛṣṇa is not cheap, if someone cannot perform it, he himself is at fault. He cannot argue that it is too difficult, for as you have shown, devotional service is our natural, constitutional position. Such an objection indicates that one has sold his soul to a cheap objective, and such a cheap mentality causes devotional service to appear too difficult. And to sell one’s soul to a cheap objective is a choice of one’s own making, for which no one but oneself can be blamed. In this way, you have destroyed sentimentality, superficiality, sense gratification, and pride, so often disguised as religion, and you have established religion for what it is - unalloyed devotion and surrender to Kṛṣṇa. But more than anything, we feel grateful to you that you have given us hope that such unalloyed devotion and surrender are not beyond us, and that, in fact, we could easily achieve them if we simply desired to have them. As an empowered personality and confidential servant of Kṛṣṇa, you have substantiated this hope by manifesting such unalloyed devotion and surrender in your own personal activities, by supporting it with self-evident, simple, yet immensely profound logic, and by constantly repeating your instructions, the essence of which is always the same: “Don’t be a fool; give up *māyā;* it’s obvious; go back to the spiritual world, back to Kṛṣṇa, it is not difficult!” For this example and these instructions, we remain forever grateful and indebted to Your Divine Grace.

## New Delhi

Until you came, O Prabhupāda, the world had gone to hell.  
Without a chance of recompense - where every fool would sell  
His life and soul for fleeting gain, bewitched by Kali’s grin  
And shackled by his own desire to lead a life of sin.  
Until you came, O Prabhupāda, our empty lives were wasted -  
An endless chain of birth and death was all we’d ever tasted.  
The bitter juice of māyā’s fruits had burned our hearts like fire,  
But then you came and brought us back to life by your desire.  
Vaikuṇṭha man in saffron cloth with skin like molten gold,  
You strode into our frozen lives and melted hearts grown cold.  
You simply preached the yuga-dharma that Lord Caitanya gave:  
”Just chant the holy name and dance - there is no other way!”

No one gave as much as you, nor will they ever give -  
You built a house of touchstone in which all the world can live.

The prophecy of Lord Caitanya you fulfilled alone -  
In ten remaining years of life, far from native home,  
You smashed the words of hollow fools who gathered in defiance  
Of common sense, who killed the soul in name of “modern science,”  
And broke the teeth of māyāvāḍis, charlatan gurus,  
And all who posed as men of God, but didn’t have a clue.  
You founded temples, gurukula, farms and BBT,  
Translated almost four-score books for all humanity,  
And gave us Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Kṛṣṇa, taught us how to cook,  
And showed us how to preach like you and distribute your books.

No one gave as much as you, nor will they ever give -  
You built a house of touchstone in which all the world can live.

So warm your voice, so sweet your glance, you smelled of sandalwood -  
And brought Vṛndāvana’s loving mood where e’r you walked or stood,  
But guiding us you sometimes changed from rose to thunderbolt  
And showed the kind of mercy that would wake us with a jolt.  
Vaikuṇṭha pastimes lay beyond the sight of shallow fools,  
Yet by your kindness even we were drowned in blissful pools  
Of nectar talks on morning walks and visits to your room,  
Quite unaware that you would leave your ISKCON all too soon.  
”This outer dress of bone and flesh is doomed to meet destruction,  
But I will never leave the one who follows my instructions -  
How much you all cooperate will show your love for me.”  
And you left us in Vṛndāvana-dhāma - in deepest ecstasy.

No one gave as much as you, nor will they ever give -  
You built a house of touchstone in which all the world can live.

O Master: AP - our shelter and our guide,  
Please help us as we spread your ISKCON movement far and wide.  
Please give us strength to see ourselves as really very small -  
Dependent on yourself and Kṛṣṇa - lest we slip and fall,  
And lose the precious vision you so freely handed down -  
That God Himself is with us in the form of sacred sound.  
His holy name, unlimited, transcends all time and space,  
But only by His mercy we received Your Divine Grace.

Some may feel that now you’re gone, there’s no one left in charge  
To carry on your lifetime’s work and save the world at large.  
For some may try to twist words for selfish motivation -  
To lose themselves and those they sway in mental speculation.  
And some may say that love and trust is lost, and leave your mission,  
Quite unaware that you’re still here to heal all cracks and schisms.  
Yet some will fear to speculate, but know just where to look -  
For everything you have to say is written in your books,  
The books to last ten thousand years - four-hundred generations -  
The Golden Age of Lord Caitanya, built on your foundation:

For no one gave as much as you, nor will they ever give -  
You built a house of touchstone in which all the world can live!

*Your servants of Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Pārthasārathi Mandira, New Delhi,*

## New Gauḍadeśa

*Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

Please accept my humble obeisances in the dust of your lotus feet.

You are the mercy incarnation of Lord Kṛṣṇa, and you have saved us from our own hands. Your words are like ambrosial nectar, and when we hear you speak we feel very happy to know that such a pure devotee as your humble self is looking after us at all times.

You are like a shepherd who very expertly tends to his flock, not letting any stray away. Despite our strong determination to first find a comfortable spot in which to relax, you are constantly there in our minds, reminding us of our real position.

You have put yourself out for souls as insignificant as ourselves, and so we want to do something for you in order to try to please you.

You have given us a glance of the real world in you *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam*. Just like the brilliant sunshine, you have lighted the path for us. Our mind is becoming clear and our intelligence is coming back.

We are your eternal servants and you are our eternal master. Please let us always remember you and never forget you, along with all your instructions.

One day we will see you again face to face. We long for that day.

Your eternal servants in New Gauḍadeśa,

## New Kurukṣetra

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Prabhupāda! How great and merciful you are!

Your greatness definitely transcends any ordinary use of the word *great* by unlimited times. People use the word *great* to describe everything from the taste of fad foods to the quality of the most common laundry detergent. Therefore, by comparison, calling you great is not very wonderful praise. But your greatness is transcendental. When we consider what you have done and who you really are, we are overwhelmed.

It was your greatness that caused you to follow the order of your spiritual master, in spite of all reverses, to preach Kṛṣṇa consciousness all over the world. If this is not greatness, then nothing else is!

The results of your preaching work are also great. Incomparable to any example, the effects of your preaching are astounding and magnificent. But the effects we perceive are almost insignificant in comparison to what the long-range effects will be. Is this not true greatness?

The message you preach is also the greatest message, without any other philosophy coming anywhere near great enough to compare to it. Indeed, to even attempt thinking of comparing other philosophies to the philosophy of Kṛṣṇa consciousness is an insult.

All your earthly pastimes are great. From your transcendental birth to the end, your activities, performed by your body, mind, or words, are uncommon and enlightening. These activities carry deep and inspiring messages to those fortunate enough to hear of them.

How great you are, the very person who has introduced the pastimes of the most munificent Personality of Godhead, the golden *avatāra* for this age, Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu. You have spread His fame around the world, and you are the sole personality to receive praise for the spreading of *saṅkīrtana* to every town and village, as prophesied.

Who can put a limit on the greatness of such an exalted Vaiṣṇava as you, who have told the world who God is, that his name is Kṛṣṇa and He is the Supreme Person? You have informed the world of the most confidential activities of the Supreme Lord, Kṛṣṇa, and have fully explained the process for becoming a participant in those activities even in this very life.

Prabhupāda! How great and merciful you are!

Considering these points only, what to speak of unlimited others, it appears to us that you are the embodiment of complete greatness.

We hope you accept this humble offering of this most auspicious day of your Vyāsa-pūjā.

Falling on the ground in the dust of your lotus feet we pray that somehow or other we can please you and thereby obtain your mercy, which even the greatest demigods are aspiring after.

By your greatness, please be merciful to us.

*Your servants at New Kurukṣetra,*
