## Taiwan

**In the Jade Green Waters of Purī**

On one great and transcendental day  
not so very long ago,  
On the golden sands of Jagannātha Purī,  
with green palms swaying on sweet, gentle breezes,  
The silver waves of the sea touched the still shore  
begging for your darśana, whispering your glories.

You wanted to take bath - where Lord Caitanya had bathed.  
The warmth and light of the sun  
reminded one that you, Śrīla Prabhupāda,  
have the full favor of Lord Kṛṣṇa.  
The kīrtana proceeded across the beach,  
and you were smiling,  
encouraging us in our devotion.  
Your loving attendants at you side  
(I had also pressed close)  
waited to serve your every desire.  
As the silver and jade waters  
massaged your lotus feet,  
We began your bath.

Brahmānanda was there - Rāmeśvara was there,  
and so were many others.  
I had missed too much mercy out of shyness  
and was determined to serve you in my presumptuous way.  
I began to bathe your golden form  
by splashing water on your back, arms and legs,  
rubbing them with my own unworthy hands.  
Your golden effulgence defeated that of the sun-god,  
and we could not tear our eyes from your form.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you were majestic!  
Just like a great rājarṣi  
who had just been victorious over all his enemies.  
And we were your humble servants,  
bathing you after a tough battle.  
Indeed, you had just waged the greatest battle of the yuga;  
you had single-handedly,  
even in the winter of your life,  
created an empire -  
with powerful armies of fierce and restless warriors,  
suitably trained to fight and destroy  
the forces of māyā.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you are our emperor -  
The commander in chief, as expert as the god of war.  
We, your devoted men, await your divine commands.  
Your men are loyal, and by their intense love  
They are willing to lay down their lives  
of material enjoyment for your sake.  
How expertly you waged war!  
How brilliant! How resourceful!  
Without you to lead us there is simply no hope.  
We see reality  
like the glint of gold in a black velvet bag.  
We are blind.

We wanted to bathe your lotus form eternally,  
but that was not to be.  
From thereafter we are meant to bathe you  
with our tears.  
I will bathe your lotus feet daily  
with the tears of my darkened eyes.  
We will serve your divine commands until our dying day.  
May our lives be consumed  
as incense offered on the altar of your instructions,  
And may the smoke be carried to your lotus feet  
on the winds of our love.  
May our prayers reach you from near and far on this day,  
On the strength of your mercy  
and the wings of your devotion.

*Your fallen servants of ISKCON Taiwan,*

**Disciples of Śrīla Prabhupāda**

Sevānanda dāsa / Yaśomatīsuta dāsa / Śravaṇīyā-devī dāsī

**Grand disciples**

Kīrtanāmṛtā-devī dāsī / Bhakta Ricardo

## Tehran

*Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

By your mercy only have we learned to recognize, appreciate, and serve Kṛṣṇa as the Supreme Personality of Godhead. Through your mercy also we have developed a loving mood of service for you, Kṛṣṇa’s pure devotee, and your representatives, such as Śrīla Kīrtanānanda Swami and Ātreya Ṛṣi prabhu. The nature of your mercy is so intense that we are receiving it despite the fact that none of us ever met Your Divine Grace personally, and most of us have never even met your more advanced disciples.

By your mercy we receive so much from you and all of your devotees. We have become fully and completely satisfied, unable to finish all the *prasādam* you have given us. We know the source of our good fortune is your compassionate mood, and the medium which carries this mercy to us is the sound vibration from the lips of your transparent followers.

We are most grateful to you for giving us these matchless gifts. We pray to Your Divine Grace to allow us to remain here in this troubled land, life after life, to preach your glories to our brothers and sisters.

*Your humble servants,*  
Devotees of Tehran Yātrā and the Karaj Farm

## Tokyo

Your friend said to you  
”You must come and see  
Śrīla Bhaktisiddhānta  
Sarasvatī.”

He was speaking of Kṛṣṇa,  
So forceful and true.  
He didn’t compromise;  
This was pleasing to you.

To broadcast this knowledge,  
He said “Go Abroad.”  
You followed his order,  
Your master and lord.

You didn’t change the order  
Or add something new.  
The perfect example  
Was shown by you.

The message of Vyāsa  
You brought with you,  
Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam  
With purports too.

You spoke the same words  
That were spoken to you,  
The words of Caitanya  
Mahāprabhu.

This place is miserable -  
It is temporary too -  
But by Kṛṣṇa’s mercy  
We came in contact with you.

We were baffled and defeated  
By māyā’s illusion,  
But because you know Kṛṣṇa  
You could remove the confusion.

You came to teach us  
Who we really are;  
To give us knowledge  
You came so far.

We are not the body  
But a minute soul.  
To serve the master  
Should be our goal.

You said “Chant Hare Kṛṣṇa  
And try simply to hear  
To destroy the illusion  
Of birth, death, old age and fear.”

A society of devotees  
Was founded by you.  
It was called ISKCON,  
With a G.B.C., too.

All over the universe  
Printing the word  
On the bṛhad-mṛdaṅga  
You will be heard.

Speaking about Kṛṣṇa  
Is your only desire  
To the conditioned souls  
In the material fire.

To describe your greatness  
Is impossible to do.  
You’re the friend of a youth  
Who is all-attractive and blue.We pray we can stay  
At your lotus feet.  
And maybe one day  
Again we can meet.

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

*Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

From the early days Japan played an important part in your preaching work. The first large quantities of *Back to Godhead* and the first *Kṛṣṇa* books and *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam* were printed here. Later, money donated by the Japanese people was very important in building your special projects in India. Even before your coming to America, you made wonderful plans for coming to Japan. You even wrote a special book for the Japanese people, *Light of the Bhāgavata*. After so many years we have finally succeeded in translating and printing this book into the Japanese language. Although it is difficult to express your extremely personal realizations of the Supreme Personality of Godhead into the language of the *śūnyavādīs,* by your unlimited desire that Kṛṣṇa consciousness be spread everywhere, Kṛṣṇa from within their hearts is giving them understanding. Now many are coming forward to surrender their lives at your lotus feet.

We feel, as Lord Nityānanda Prabhu and Śrīla Haridāsa Ṭhākura felt about Jagai and Mādhāi, that as Japanese people become Kṛṣṇa conscious, it is special proof of your unlimited potencies for spreading Kṛṣṇa consciousness, and this will increase your fame and the worldwide acceptance of Kṛṣṇa consciousness as the solution to all problems even more.

We pray that you will make us your sincere followers, for there is no other formula for success in Kṛṣṇa consciousness. Please forgive us for being so slow to take up your mood and so inept at carrying out your orders. You have given the *Light of the Bhāgavata* to dissipate the darkness of this iron age of Kali. But we are blinded by the effulgence and unable to always see things properly. Please give us your vision so that we do not stray from the path laid out by you.

*Your most fallen aspiring servants in Japan,*

**Disciples of Śrīla Prabhupāda**

## Toronto

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

*Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

In the current world there is a tremendous increase of perverse affairs, and a massive cloud of pessimism pervades the atmosphere. It has become commonplace to identify more with Satan than a saint or relate more to a vampire than a Vaikuṇṭha man. With “no God in control” and everything evolving out of lust, as atheists do voice, and with a strong obsession for the flesh, it is most evident that the world has entered a very dismal time.

Factually the material world is a very gloomy place, as confirmed by Lord Kṛṣṇa in the *Gītā*. But contrary to all this negative energy is the descent of Kṛṣṇa’s agent, the Vaikuṇṭha man, who is so influential that he can surcharge everything with spiritual vitality, bringing on new hope and delivering a basic feeling of optimism.

At one particular time, as the *Bhāgavatam* portrayed, the earth was unproductive and dull; but Pṛthu Mahārāja, the Supreme Personality, drove it to yield all kinds of bounties, and in this way he revealed the earth’s potential. As you gave the example, one may find a discarded gourd somewhere and a string elsewhere. Assemble the two, and you have something useful - a stringed instrument which plays music. So similarly, you saw some morose-looking hippies here, other delinquents to Kṛṣṇa’s rule there, and who were all practically useless, and you grouped them together to form a most positive blessing to the world - the International Society for Kṛṣṇa Consciousness.

This ISKCON movement, which is a branch of the Caitanya tree, the tree of pure love of God, will remain a limb so long as its members are eager to spread Lord Caitanya’s mercy and remain rigid in prosecuting the principles of sinless life.

As a branch of the branch of the Caitanya tree, the inmates of New Remuṇā Dhāma humbly beg for your mercy to force us to become most helpful in inundating Kṛṣṇa consciousness to this portion of the dingy material existence, thus serving your dearmost Bhaktivedanta Book Trust.

Thank you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, for being that Vaikuṇṭha man.

May we remain dust particles at your lotus feet forever.

*Your servants at New Remuṇā Dhāma, ISKCON Toronto,*

## Trinidad

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

*Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

Please accept my humble obeisances. All glories, all glories to your blessed Vyāsa-pūjā. Kindly allow me to eternally assist in glorifying your transcendental appearance.

The most wonderful and glorious thing about this auspicious day is knowing that you boldly organized ISKCON by the greatest and most auspicious activity, book distribution. This movement was started by book distribution, and it could only be maintained by book distribution.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, even though we understand that this book distribution is your mercy, we continually ask for your blessings, forgetting that if we gave all our attention and energy to book distribution we inherit unlimited blessings.

You also taught us the meaning of Vyāsa-pūjā, which is to satisfy your wishes. Every day could be a Vyāsa-pūjā day, real Vyāsa-pūjā being book distribution. For this event we should especially make a big marathon to send more *lakṣmī* to B.B.T. (your heart), thereby increasing your joy.

O Śrīla Prabhupāda, my deepest wish is to distribute your books to the world, for by reading your purports, peace and prosperity will be theirs. You set the example; we are the proof. You lived for this purpose, and wishing to follow in your footsteps, I dedicate my meager existence. The Pandavas received the benediction of Lord Kṛṣṇa for serving His instructions with faith and determination, giving up all doubts and speculations. So, too, by distributing your books, we will gain perfection.

My respectful obeisances unto you! O my *Guru* Mahārāja, you appeared on this auspicious day to bring peace and harmony to the world, fulfilling the desires of great *ācāryas* like your spiritual master, Śrīla Bhaktisiddhānta Sarasvatī, and Śrīla Bhaktivinoda Ṭhākura. As for us, your disciples, we simply have to give your books to the world, thus establishing your glories, though your reject personal glorification. But wasn’t Mādhavendra Purī glorified even in Jagannātha Purī though he fled such glorification in the market of Remuṇā village for being favored by Lord Gopīnātha with a pot of sweet rice? Therefore, even though you are simply executing the orders of your spiritual master, we strive to give our energy to your instructions. Then the day will come when, after receiving your transcendental literatures and sincerely applying the instructions for *bhakti-yoga* found in your purports, the whole world will sing out and glorify your transcendental personality. Jaya Prabhupāda, jaya Prabhupāda, jaya Prabhupāda!

*We remain, your petty book distributors of Trinidad,*

## Vancouver

*Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

Please accept my humble obeisances at your lotus feet. Śrīla Prabhupāda, you are the most perfect devotee. You fully manifest the twenty-six qualities of a pure soul. You are a *śaktyāveśa-avatāra,* empowered by Kṛṣṇa to descend from His abode and reclaim the forgetful conditioned souls. From birth you manifested perfect Kṛṣṇa consciousness. You yourself once declared to some disciples, “never was there a time when I did not know Kṛṣṇa.” Who can estimate your glories, and what fool would dare to compare himself to you? You have descended to fulfill the prophecy of Lord Caitanya that this chanting of Hare Kṛṣṇa would resound in every town and village in the world. You have delivered to this world in its darkest hour over fifty large books on the science of God, which are the framework for ISKCON and the law books for mankind for the next ten thousand years. Christianity has spread widely on the basis of one book with scanty information about the Personality of Godhead. Christ has become celebrated, although he withheld intimate information about the spiritual world and taught the rogues sub religious principles for elevation to the mode of goodness. But you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, have so expertly presented the sublime teachings of Lord Caitanya which reveal the most intimate relationships that the living entities can have with the Supreme Person and have shown by personal example the step-by-step practical process for attaining that goal. You have taken the highest philosophical understanding of Kṛṣṇa and through your purity and wisdom made it available, understandable, and tangible, even for the ordinary man in the street. You are to ISKCON what Jesus Christ is to Christianity. When one speaks of Christianity one immediately thinks of Christ, and similarly when one talks about ISKCON one immediately remembers Śrīla Prabhupāda. You are inseparable from ISKCON. You are the founder; you are the guiding light. Your position will not diminish with the passage of time, but will grow as more and more the world wakes up to acknowledge and appreciate the extraordinary personality who walked among us to revitalize real religious principles in the spiritual wasteland, the desert of Kali-yuga. Out of your causeless compassion you have picked us up out of our wretched condition. Śrīla Prabhupāda, you are the faultless and pure; however, you once said your one mistake, your one fault, was to accept so many disciples who were not qualified. Actually this is not a fault, this is the greatest display of compassion. In *The Nectar of Devotion* you have described compassion: “a person who is unable to bear another’s distress is called compassionate.” In one letter to an early disciple who had observed that many of the new disciples who had been initiated had left, you replied, “Yes, I am prepared to go to hell for Lord Caitanya.” You are *para-duḥkha-duḥkhī.* You have agreed to take so much trouble and tolerate so much discomfort simply to save us. In order to initiate us and start us on the spiritual path, you agreed to suffer the reactions of accepting the disciples’ *karma*, and for that you would sometimes become ill. You knew full well that many of your disciples would leave Kṛṣṇa consciousness and take up nonsense activities again, but you accepted them anyway, just to start them on the path back to Godhead. Whatever small amount of service they rendered you will be their eventful passport to the spiritual world. It is stated in the *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam* (8.7.44):

*tapyante loka-tāpena*  
*sādhavaḥ prāyaśo janāḥ*  
*paramārādhanaṁ tad dhi*  
*puruṣasyākhilātmanaḥ*

**”It is said that great personalities almost always accept voluntary suffering because of the suffering of the people in general. This is considered the highest method of worshiping the Supreme Personality of Godhead, who is present in everyone’s heart.”**

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you have taken me from the lowest position in life: miserable, apathetic, drug-crazed debauchee, and raised me to the highest platform, that of a devotee of Kṛṣṇa. You have given us hope. In this position our anxieties have diminished and we have become glorious.

It has been our tendency in life to exploit everyone - parents, friends, lovers - all of them we have exploited for our sense gratification. They are like victims we use for our enjoyment. Unworthy as we are, we must not carry this same mentality into our relationship with you. You have taken us from our fallen condition and uplifted us. Feeling some relief from material distress, if we become proud or forgetful of your instructions, again we will become a mouse!

Thinking of your transcendental qualities we have become ashamed at our lack of sincerity. Please forgive our many offenses. We are praying to you, who have so kindly extended your mercy, to now please give us the intelligence to take it fully and hold on, so that we may become qualified and worthy disciples of Your Divine Grace.

All glories to Śrīla Prabhupāda!

## Veracruz

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

**I offer my respectful obeisances unto His Divine Grace A.C. Bhaktivedanta Swami Prabhupāda, who is very dear to Lord Kṛṣṇa, having taken shelter at His lotus feet. Our respectful obeisances are unto you, O spiritual master, servant of Sarasvatī Gosvāmī. You are kindly preaching the message of Lord Caitanyadeva and delivering the Western countries, which are filled with impersonalism and nihilism.”**

O divine Śrīla Prabhupāda! Please accept my humble obeisances prostrated in the shade of your divine lotus feet.

O Jagad-*guru*, divine spiritual master of the universe, I do not have any good qualities to address you and glorify you appropriately, because your divine qualities are limitless and only can be sung by great souls who are your pure devotees and who have been empowered by you completely in order to make this world Kṛṣṇa conscious.

O Śrīla Prabhupāda! Your divine appearance, activities, pastimes, and also your disappearance are transcendental, and the only purpose of your advent is to give your causeless mercy to all the conditioned souls of this world, who find themselves suffering terrible anxiety, without any hope for escaping the torments of this material existence. O Jagad-*guru*! It is just out of your causeless mercy that you have expanded yourself into the present *ācāryas* for spreading Kṛṣṇa consciousness.

In this way those who were not able to appreciate your great transcendental appearance and your pastimes in this world will be able to have a spiritual father who can take them back home, back to Godhead, and also toward you, our eternal spiritual grandfather.

My only desire is to have my consciousness purified by the words emanating from your lotus mouth.

O Śrīla Prabhupāda! We do not know how to repay the unlimited mercy that you kindly give to us. The greatest acknowledgement that I can demonstrate would be to purify my heart from the desires of sense gratification and give it to the divine lotus feet of your eternal servants: my spiritual master and father, Śrīla Hṛdayānanda dāsa Goswami.

Let me offer my humble and prostrated obeisances unto his lotus feet with great respect and veneration, on the auspicious day of your glorious appearance.

All Glories to you!  
All Glories to Śrīla Prabhupāda!  
Jaya Jagad-*guru* Patita-pāvana  
Śrīla Prabhupāda Ki Jaya!

*The most insignificant servant of your servant,*

**Grand disciples**

Kavi dāsa / Nṛsiṁha Caitanya dāsa / Daśaratha dāsa / Paramātmā dāsa

## Vienna

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

*Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

On this auspicious day of your appearance,  
demigods in heaven,  
great saints, and saintly persons in this universe  
all are praising your unfathomable glories,  
O Supreme Personality of Servitor Godhead.

Anyone who understands  
that your appearance and activities are transcendental,  
that you appear and disappear according to Lord Kṛṣṇa’s supreme will,  
That you descend from the spiritual kingdom  
simply to punish the demons,  
protect the devotees and reestablish the principles of religion,  
will upon leaving his body return back home, back to Godhead.

Just like Lord Caitanya Mahāprabhu,  
who while walking through the forests of Jhārikhaṇḍa,  
made wild animals dance and chant Hare Kṛṣṇa,  
Your Divine Grace,  
while walking through the degraded jungle like cities  
of Kali-yuga America, Europe, and Australasia,  
made wild, animalistic hippies into pure devotees,  
who are presently chanting in ecstasy  
Hare Kṛṣṇa Hare Kṛṣṇa Kṛṣṇa Kṛṣṇa Hare Hare  
Hare Rāma Hare Rāma Rāma Rāma Hare Hare.

Just like Lord Nityānanda,  
who while preaching in Bengal  
simply destroyed all demoniac philosophies  
and blessed everyone and all with pure love of Kṛṣṇa,  
Your Divine Grace,  
in the form of your teachings,  
is destroying all demoniac and atheistic philosophies of our modern world,  
and simultaneously, your blessings are causing the most wretched,  
fallen souls to experience love for God,  
the matchless gift.

Just like Śrī Advaita, who is Mahā-Viṣṇu Ananta,  
you have caused Lord Caitanya Mahāprabhu to descend,  
manifesting within your ISKCON movement,  
which is nondifferent from your transcendental form.

Just as Gadādhara and Śrīvāsa,  
you are eternally enjoying the amṛta-rasa  
of Lord Caitanya’s ecstatic saṅkīrtana,  
with mṛdaṅgas, karatālas,  
in the association of unlimited devotees.

During your kīrtanas,  
like in Tompkins Square,  
you caused the whole Pañca-tattva to appear.  
Every time you chant, our dear savior and master Śrīla Prabhupāda,  
Śrī Kṛṣṇa Caitanya, Prabhu Nityānanda, Śrī Advaita, Gadādhara,  
Śrīvāsa and all the gaura-bhakta-vṛndas appear.  
In order to spread saṅkīrtana movement,  
the pañca-tattva danced and chanted  
and distributed love of God to everyone,  
thus plundering the storehouse of love of God,  
which was sealed till then.

Your Divine Grace  
is presently distributing love of God  
in the form of your transcendental books.  
You saṅkīrtana devotees are continuously plundering the storehouses  
and warehouses of the BBT,  
thus causing a flood of transcendental literature,  
which will inundate the whole world, causing everyone to drown  
in the nectarean oceans of your Bhaktivedanta purports  
to increase unlimitedly. At this precise moment, someone is receiving your book.  
Who can estimate the extent of your mercy?

*Your fallen and unqualified servants on the Austrian SKP*

## Vṛndāvana

*Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

Hare Kṛṣṇa. Please accept our most humble obeisances unto the dust of your lotus like beautiful feet. All glories to Your Divine Grace, savior of the whole world, who has spread the holy name of Lord Kṛṣṇa all over the world and who is very kind upon the fallen souls like myself.

Today is the most blessed event, your auspicious appearance day. So many people around the world are taking birth and dying, which has no meaning. But you appearance is unique. You only appeared to save the fallen souls. The whole world is gliding down towards hell very quickly, but you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, have saved the world. Your ISKCON is the only hope; otherwise, there is no hope. All of us were suffering in this material world, futilely trying to gratify our senses. But you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, gave us the real understanding by which we could end our suffering. You explained very logically that we are different from our bodies. We are souls, and we should act for the soul. Then only can we attain real happiness and peace, not by acting for the body.

Acting for the body is just like a fish trying to enjoy out of water. You may put it in an air-conditioned room and give it so many dollars and everything of this material world, but it won’t be happy, because its life is in the water. In the same way, we can only be happy if we attach ourselves to the lotus feet of Śrī Kṛṣṇa and become faithful servants of Śrī Kṛṣṇa. By your causeless mercy, Śrīla Prabhupāda, so many people around the world have become peaceful and happy. Actually, this is the only way to become happy, which none of us knew previously. Who can be more merciful than you Śrīla Prabhupāda? No one! If there was anyone other than you, he could have come to the public and saved the fallen souls; but no one did that. You are the only one, Śrīla Prabhupāda; you are the only one.

In India, people used to read the *Bhagavad-gītā,* but they did not know the real meaning of *Bhagavad-gītā*, because they were not ready to accept Lord Śrī Kṛṣṇa as the Supreme Personality of Godhead. But you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, on the basis of *Bhagavad-gītā,* have explained that no one should be worshiped other than Śrī Kṛṣṇa. Without understanding Śrī Kṛṣṇa, our repetition of birth and death cannot be stopped. Only Kṛṣṇa can stop it. Śrīla Prabhupāda, who gave us this most confidential knowledge? Only you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, no one else. Who can be like you, Śrīla Prabhupāda? No one. By giving the knowledge of soul, Super soul, and Śrī Kṛṣṇa, you have saved us. We are feeling very happy by having the knowledge of the soul. By your causeless mercy, we are trying to follow the knowledge of the soul and Super soul. And those who are following this seriously and sincerely, they are really feeling a difference. Their life is completely different. They are very happy. Their happiness is increasing day by day. Those who are true followers of Your Divine Grace, they are really very happy and peaceful. I have seen personally that your only desire was that the whole world should take to this Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement and become happy and peaceful.

The only way to spread Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement is by distributing your most beautiful and valuable literature throughout the whole world. You always wanted us to distribute more and more literature. Whoever gets this literature (what to speak of reading it, even by touching these books) will get purification. This is a fact. Once a devotee in Canada wrote you, “Śrīla Prabhupāda, we are traveling and distributing your literature. Should we carry Deities with us to worship?” You replied, “You need not carry the Deities to worship; you worship my literature.” Of course, your books are woshipable because they are the most beautiful books in the world.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, I have one prayer unto you lotus feet if you will please accept it. That is, please empower the residents of Vṛndāvana-dhāma to distribute more and more books and allow us to become an instrument in your great movement. If we can’t become this, then our life is the greatest failure. Please allow us to serve you. Vṛndāvana is your home; you choose to reside here eternally. We have to keep your house according to your desire. Your desire is that your home should be well managed. Whoever visits Krishna Balaram temple should go back with some inspiration. More and more of your books should be distributed. Please empower us to do this. *Kṛṣṇa-śakti vinā tāre nahi paravartana.* Without your power, we cannot do anything.

Thank you very much for allowing us to be in this most magnificent Krishna Balaram Mandir. We hope one day by your causeless mercy we will understand the importance of being here in Śrī Vṛndāvana-dhāma.  
Jaya Śrīla Prabhupāda! All glories to Your Divine Grace.

*Your fallen servants at Śrī Vṛndāvana-dhāma,*

**Disciples of Śrīla Prabhupāda**

## Washington, D.C.

*Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

Please accept our humble obeisances at your lotus feet. Devotional service is described by Śrīla Rūpa Gosvāmī as very rarely achieved and the only means of attracting Kṛṣṇa. We also hear that it gives immediate relief from material distress. By seeing your life and hearing your words, the minds of millions have become peaceful and their lives meaningful. You have stated that the supreme occupation for all humanity is that by which men can attain to loving devotional service unto the transcendental Lord. Such devotional service must be unmotivated and uninterrupted to completely satisfy the self. You have made a perfect plan for each individual to be engaged on this platform of *ahaitukī apratihatā* through your *saṅkīrtana* program. Life becomes free from distress, Kṛṣṇa becomes attracted, and the conditioned soul achieves a position in devotional service. You told me in my brahminical initiation letter that not even nuclear war could stop *saṅkīrtana*, because it is transcendental. This *saṅkīrtana* is the highest *dharma.* By your mercy, we are able to act on the platform of previous sages, concerning ourselves with the welfare of others and offering a solution to the world problems. Now our assignment is in the most important city in the world. You have stated that America can change the world by its example. We are praying to you to give us the strength and the ability to distribute more of your books than ever before in our nation’s capital.

*Your loving devotees and grand disciples,*

**Disciples of Śrīla Prabhupāda**

Mahāmuni dāsa / *Guru*-vāgmī dāsa

## Wellington

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

*Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

By your great mercy, knowledge of Kṛṣṇa is flooding this world. The *Bhāgavata Purāṇa,* carried by you, is now shedding light on our dark lives. Thus the suffering of the people is being mitigated.

Your selfless endeavor to fulfill your spiritual master’s order has endeared you to the Supreme Lord, Śrī Kṛṣṇa, and therefore to everyone else also.

The multifarious benedictions of Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu, offered to the world through your International Society for Kṛṣṇa Consciousness, are ever-increasing testimonies to your greatness.

From the hearts of pure men a deep gratitude and love is pouring forth on this, your Vyāsa-pūjā day.

We here in the Wellington Yātrā feel greatly privileged to be receiving your blessings, and we pray that we can give every moment of our lives to glorifying you as the savior of the world.

*Your servants,*

**Grand disciples**

Subala dāsa / Bhaktilatā / Puṇḍarīkākṣam dāsa / Guñjāmāla dāsa / Pāda-padma-śubha-śuci-devī dāsī / Bhakta Frank / Bhakta Barry / Bhakta Charles

## West Germany

*Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

Since time immemorial, the pure devotees of the Lord have been celebrated for their unparalleled heroic feats done in the service of the Supreme Personality of Godhead. The Vedic scriptures loudly proclaim the glories of these great souls as being unique in the history of the world, for the powers they exhibit are directly coming from Kṛṣṇa. Thus, like Kṛṣṇa, the *janma* and *karma* of such pure devotees is completely transcendental.

For instance, when the Lord appeared in ancient times as Śrī Rāmacandra, His foremost devotee was the mighty Hanumān. But Hanumān could not be recognized as being the best of Vaiṣṇavas before he met Lord Rāma and he began to serve Him directly. Until that time he was considered to be nothing more than an ordinary monkey dwelling in the forest. When the opportunity for devotional service arose, however, the very special significance of Hanumān’s eternal relationship to Lord Rāma became quickly and dramatically visible to all.

Similarly, the life of His Divine Grace A.C. Bhaktivedanta Swami Prabhupāda, before he embarked upon the great mission to fulfill the order of Śrī Vārṣabhānavī-devī dāsa Śrīla Bhaktisiddhānta Sarasvatī Ṭhākura, seems quite ordinary. No one among his God brothers could imagine the great feats Śrīla Prabhupāda would perform for Śrī Śrī *Guru* and Gaurāṅga.

Hanumān first revealed his qualities as the greatest *Vaiṣṇava* devotee of Lord Rāma when he took up the task of leaping across the sea from India to Laṅkā in order to see if Sītā was being held captive there. This was a feat unmatched in history. It was fraught with many dangers. But Hanumān fearlessly accomplished the task, reaching Laṅkā in a single bound. By that act alone his fame was established for all time.

Similarly, Śrīla Prabhupāda, you established the uniqueness of your position among the Vaiṣṇavas of this age when you left the shores of India on the steamer *Jaladuta.* This was no less a demanding and treacherous challenge than that faced by Hanumān thousands of years ago. Fearlessly, in your old age, you braved a stormy sea and a heart attack just to satisfy the desire of your spiritual master. Hanumān, too, had to face tossing waves and was nearly devoured by a huge *timiṅgila* sea-monster. Thus, Śrīla Prabhupāda, by your crossing of the Atlantic to America your fame as a preacher of Śrī Caitanya’s movement will forever be recorded in the annals of wonderful accomplishments in the service of the Lord.

When Hanumān landed at Laṅkā, he, possessing great intelligence and all mystic powers, reduced his body to the size of a cat just so that he could search for Mother Sītā without being seen by the *rākṣasa* guards of the evil demon Rāvaṇa. Silently and unobtrusively, he searched high and low for the goddess Lakṣmī-devī, who had appeared in this world as Sītā, consort of *kṛṣṇa-avatāra* Śrī Rāmacandra. Hanumān even entered the opulent quarters of Rāvaṇa, but in no way was he interested in the beautiful queens who lay asleep there. His mind was dedicated to finding Sītā-devī. At last he saw her, forlorn and alone, held under guard in a palace garden. From a hidden place he spoke to her of the glories of Rāma. Thus Sītā’s love for the Lord overflowed, and she became bright with joy.

Similarly, when you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, first arrived in that latter-day kingdom of Rāvaṇa, the United States, you immediately assumed the humble guise of a poor, harmless Indian mendicant who would be visiting for only a few months. No one among the demons could imagine your real intent. You were, like Hanumān, searching high and low for the goddess Lakṣmī.

Lakṣmī-devī is the personification of devotion to Lord Viṣṇu. She is also the goddess of prosperity, who naturally graces those who are pure servants of the Lord. In your days of preaching Kṛṣṇa consciousness in Jhansi during the early 1950s, you noticed that Mother Lakṣmī had seemingly abandoned India. The Indian people were unable to muster up the *bhakti* required to take to your message with complete conviction. Furthermore, your preaching work was hampered by lack of funds. So you journeyed to USA to find Lakṣmī and engage her again in the service of Kṛṣṇa.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, as you moved almost unnoticed through the streets of New York City, you studied with a sharp eye the characteristics of the degraded Kali-yuga culture, but you were unmoved by its allurements. Other so-called swamis had come to New York before you, only to join forces with Rāvaṇa, put on Western clothing, drink, smoke, eat meat, and sport with young girls. But you shunned all this, pushing onward in your mission. At last, the mother of devotion graced your efforts. A few young people, your first disciples, came to you, morose and neglected, looking for shelter. With the sweet vibration of *kṛṣṇa-kathā* you caused their devotion to bloom. You were fond of recounting how one astonished priest remarked that your disciples were “bright-faced” - something unknown in the Western world. With the flowering of *kṛṣṇa-bhakti* in the USA followed the other aspects of Mother Lakṣmī’s blessings - wealth. Thus your preaching efforts began to expand geometrically across the face of the earth.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, like Hanumān you then assumed a mighty stature, and by your strong preaching you began to devastate Western materialism’s pretentious golden city of dreams. The demons were powerless to stop you. You crossed the ocean again to India, the land of Rāma and Kṛṣṇa, bringing back the good news: Devotion to the Lord is not dead! Thus in India mighty temples sprang up - Māyāpur, Vṛndāvan, Bombay and Hyderabad - all as a result of your preaching work, which left the people of India open-mouthed in astonishment. You amassed an army of low-born preachers of Kṛṣṇa consciousness, and you stood at its head as the most prominent devotee of Lord Gaura-Nitāi. Through book production and book distribution you engaged your followers in building a bridge of Kṛṣṇa consciousness that links all nations of the modern world in a transcendental network of *saṅkīrtana*, just as Hanumān organized the building of the bridge to Laṅkā for Rāma and Lakṣmaṇa.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, your divine pastimes are so numerous that it is impossible to even begin to do justice to them in this meager offering. We are simply trying to point out that, considering the history of devotional service as revealed in the Vedic scriptures, we find your accomplishments of only twelve years of worldwide preaching to be of truly epic proportions. A great scripture of many volumes has already been compiled as an attempt to describe your glories. But your pastimes as the foremost Vaiṣṇava among Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu’s army preachers have by no means ended.

Now, Śrīla Prabhupāda, you are leading your loyal Vaiṣṇava soldiers across your bridge of *bhāgavata-dharma* to completely destroy the forces of atheism and degradation on this planet. We feel certain that soon the Supreme Lord, Śrī Kṛṣṇa, and His eternal consort, Śrīmatī Rādhārāṇī, will be established as the only worshipable Deities for all humanity. At that time, when Their Lordships are worshiped as the world īśvara by teeming masses of devotees of all nationalities, we will long to see your triumphant return as Their Lordships foremost servant. We long to see you given the seat of honor and reverence at Their lotus feet, and we love to worship you with all paraphernalia and offerings.

Jaya Śrīla Prabhupāda!

*Your servants of ISCKON Germany*

Your devotees are first class,

We want to touch people with the fragrance of your lotus feet by having a full set of your books standing proudly in their living rooms. Then they will feel the same nectar we felt in your lectures and darśanas, and continue to feel in your service - that ecstatic feeling of security a child feels at home protected by his father.

You are home to us, Śrīla Prabhupāda, and you are our eternal father. If we sold one of your books for a million dollars, it would be too cheap. Actually, your books can only be “purchased” by full surrender.

Little do the conditioned souls realize that, by your mercy, the few hundred or a thousand dollars they pay for a full set is just a down payment - a down payment on their first-class ticket to your first class lotus feet.
